from re_arc.dsl import *
def generate_1fad071e() -> dict:
    diff_lb = 0
    diff_ub = 1
    print(f"diff_lb: {diff_lb}, diff_ub: {diff_ub}")
    
    cols = remove(1, interval(0, 10, 1))
    print(f"cols: {cols}")
    
    h = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (10, 30))
    print(f"h: {h}")
    
    w = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (10, 30))
    print(f"w: {w}")
    
    nbl = randint(0, 5)
    print(f"nbl: {nbl}")
    
    nobjs = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (nbl, max(nbl, (h * w) // 10)))
    print(f"nobjs: {nobjs}")
    
    bgc, otherc = sample(cols, 2)
    print(f"bgc: {bgc}, otherc: {otherc}")
    
    succ = 0
    print(f"succ: {succ}")
    
    tr = 0
    print(f"tr: {tr}")
    
    maxtr = 5 * nobjs
    print(f"maxtr: {maxtr}")
    
    bcount = 0
    print(f"bcount: {bcount}")
    
    gi = canvas(bgc, (h, w))
    print(f"gi: {gi}")
    
    inds = asindices(gi)
    print(f"inds: {inds}")
    
    ofcfrbinds = {1: set(), otherc: set()}
    print(f"ofcfrbinds: {ofcfrbinds}")
    
    while succ < nobjs and tr < maxtr:
        tr += 1
        print(f"tr (after increment): {tr}")
        
        col = choice((1, otherc))
        print(f"col: {col}")
        
        oh = randint(1, 3)
        print(f"oh: {oh}")
        
        ow = randint(1, 3)
        print(f"ow: {ow}")
        
        if bcount < nbl:
            col = 1
            oh, ow = 2, 2
            print(f"Adjusted col: {col}, oh: {oh}, ow: {ow}")
        else:
            while col == 1 and oh == ow == 2:
                col = choice((1, otherc))
                print(f"Adjusted col in while: {col}")
                oh = randint(1, 3)
                print(f"Adjusted oh in while: {oh}")
                ow = randint(1, 3)
                print(f"Adjusted ow in while: {ow}")
        
        bd = backdrop(frozenset({(0, 0), (oh - 1, ow - 1)}))
        print(f"bd: {bd}")
        
        cands = sfilter(inds, lambda ij: ij[0] <= h - oh and ij[1] <= w - ow)
        print(f"cands: {cands}")
        
        if len(cands) == 0:
            continue
        
        loc = choice(totuple(cands))
        loci, locj = loc
        print(f"loc: {loc}, loci: {loci}, locj: {locj}")
        
        bd = shift(bd, loc)
        print(f"bd (after shift): {bd}")
        
        if bd.issubset(inds) and len(mapply(dneighbors, bd) & ofcfrbinds[col]) == 0:
            succ += 1
            print(f"succ (after increment): {succ}")
            
            inds = inds - bd
            print(f"inds (after subtraction): {inds}")
            
            ofcfrbinds[col] = ofcfrbinds[col] | mapply(dneighbors, bd) | bd
            print(f"ofcfrbinds (after update): {ofcfrbinds}")
            
            gi = fill(gi, col, bd)
            print(f"gi (after fill): {gi}")
            
            if col == 1 and oh == ow == 2:
                bcount += 1
                print(f"bcount (after increment): {bcount}")
    
    return {'input': gi}
